| Kohane | (What's a menial rehearsal?) | talk |

| Minori | Kohane? | talk |

| Minori | Well... I've been watching a DVD of this particular idol recently. The singing and dancing is so cute! | talk |
| Minori | So a menial rehearsal is where I think about what it would look like if I'm the one singing and dancing instead. | talk |
| Kohane | Oh... So you don't actually sing and dance while you're doing that? | talk |

| Minori | Um, there's something I'd like to discuss with all of you. Mind if we talk while we rest? | talk |
| Haruka | Sure, what's up? | talk |
| Minori | I'd like to know more about what we need to do and what kind of schedule we're looking at if we really do want to put on an event. | talk |
| Minori | I have a vague idea of what goes on during meet and greets, mini-concerts and when CDs go on sale, but I don't really know what happens before all that. | talk |
| Haruka | I see.<br>We haven't really talked about that much, have we? | talk |
| Haruka | Oh, also... I may not be the best person to ask since we're looking to do a much more modest event. | talk |
| Airi | Yeah. ASRUN's nationwide tours saw tens of thousands of fans gather for each event, right? | talk |
| Minori | That's right! What we need to do is gonna change based on the scale of our event. | talk |
| Airi | Bullseye! | talk |
| Airi | We could use the mini-concert I did at the mall with QT as an example. Wanna know more about that? | talk |
| Shizuku | Oh, that means we get to hear all about what happened behind the scenes that time. | talk |
| Minori | Yes, please! | talk |
| Airi | Okay, let's start with how our manager told us that we'd be a mini-concert to celebrate our CD going on sale about two months in advance. | talk |
| Airi | Our agency, along with the production company that helped us plan everything, pretty much focused on the core of the event, like sales quotas for CDs and where we'd be performing.  | talk |
| Haruka | Big or small, it's important to have a set goal in mind when preparing for an event. | talk |
| Minori | Right. Lemme jot that down... | talk |
| Shizuku | How did you practice for it? | talk |
| Airi | All the QT members would usually meet after work and stuff. | talk |
| Airi | Also, there was only so much we could do in a day, but we also had two months to prepare for it. | talk |
| Airi | On the day of, we met with the staff, our manager, our contacts from the mall... | talk |
| Airi | Oh, the lead from the production company, some of their event staff and the MC too... So like seven or eight people in total. | talk |
| Minori | Th-That sounds like so few! | talk |
| Airi | Actually, we didn't even need that many. I mean, they even found an MC who could set the stage for us. | talk |
| Haruka | They're usually very good at drawing everybody in, especially the people at the front, and warming up the crowd by doing light introductions for everyone. | talk |
| Airi | Yeah, they can make a huge difference. | talk |
| Shizuku | Come to think of it, Cheerful * Days usually had an MC do that for us at the start of our shows when we were just debuting. | talk |
| Haruka | It sounds like both of your groups' agencies really wanted you to succeed. I mean, it's pretty rare to have an MC for a mini-concert like that. | talk |
| Minori | Yes, I see... | talk |
| Minori | Oh, I have a question about setting everything up at a venue. Does the staff take care of all of that? | talk |
| Airi | Yeah, they do.<br>But that's not all. | talk |
| Airi | On top of having to move everybody around in an orderly manner, they have to make sure people aren't blocking any paths. | talk |
| Airi | And once a show's over, they have to help sell CDs and merch. That staff takes care of all the crowd control so we can focus on our meet and greet. | talk |
| Shizuku | Setting up a venue, crowd control, clearing paths, selling CDs and merch... They all juggle so much at once. | talk |
| Airi | Yeah, which is why we can get away with having just a few for an event. | talk |

| Haruka | Okay. Nice work, everyone. | talk |

| Minori | Oh, there's something I need to ask before I forget. Would you all mind if we talked while we stretch? | talk |
| Shizuku | Yes, Minori? | talk |
| Minori | So, now that we've decided to accept that offer to appear on iHaz... | talk |
| Minori | I know I might be getting ahead of myself here... | talk |
| Minori | But I want you all to teach me how to look good on camera! | talk |
| Shizuku | How to look good on camera? | talk |
| Minori | Y-Yeah... I have a habit of blinking or looking the wrong direction whenever I get pictures of me taken... | talk |
| Minori | Which is why I wanna learn how to look better when a camera is pointed towards me! | talk |
| Airi | I see. You're right that you're getting a little ahead of yourself, but I think we can give you a few tips. | talk |
| Minori | Yay! Thank you so much! | talk |
| Airi | You got it!<br>First, these shoots always involve a lot of cameras. | talk |
| Airi | So, it's important to check how you fit into each camera's frame. | talk |
| Minori | How I fit into each frame? | talk |
| Haruka | For example, camera one might be pointed at you head-on, while camera two is looking at you from the side. You could be gone from camera three because it's looking at the people next to you. | talk |
| Shizuku | Where a camera is can greatly change how you appear in the end. | talk |
| Airi | Exactly. There are cases where one camera's zoomed in on someone but they sort of waste their chance by looking and speaking in a completely different direction.  | talk |
| Airi | However, it's not always easy to do those checks since everyone's usually scrambling around to get ready. | talk |
| Airi | Which is why you usually have to just imagine how you're gonna look based on where the cameras are located. | talk |
| Minori | O-Okay, I see... That seems kinda tricky. | talk |
| Haruka | We have some time, so why don't we practice that a little? | talk |
| Minori | Huh? You can practice that? | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah. We'll just be using the cameras on our phones, but it should give you a better idea. | talk |
| Minori | Oh, okay! Yes, please! | talk |
| Shizuku | Hehe. I'll be more than happy to help. | talk |
| Airi | Same. It's important to get used to this, so let's give it a go. | talk |
| Minori | Alright, let's do it! | talk |

| Haruka | Oh, you're not sure how to study for Modern Japanese? | talk |
| Minori | Yeah... | talk |
| Minori | For history, all the important stuff is usually bolded, so it's pretty easy to know what I need to memorize. | talk |
| Minori | Also, for Classical Japanese, I just need to memorize vocab and be able to explain what certain phrases and passages mean... | talk |
| Airi | Yeah, now I see what you mean. Modern Japanese isn't as cut and dry. | talk |
| Minori | Exactly!<br>That's why I wanna know how you guys study for it! | talk |
| Haruka | Well... For essays and stories, I try to see things from the writer or characters' perspectives. | talk |
| Haruka | With novels in particular, I feel that it's important to dissect why a character's feelings change and in what way. | talk |
| Minori | Oh, right! | talk |
| Minori | They always ask that on tests.<br>“Why did the protagonist feel the way they did?” or, “What led them to do what they did?” and stuff! | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah. Aside from that, memorizing the kanji and idioms being used might be good. | talk |
| Minori | Lemme jot that down...<br>Thanks, Haruka! | talk |
| Minori | Next, Airi, please tell me how you study for this subject! | talk |
| Airi | I really don't do anything that special.<br>Like Haruka said, I try to memorize the kanji, idioms, phrases and... | talk |
| Airi | Oh, pronouns and stuff. You, they, him, her, this and that. Keeping an eye out for who's pointing to what is supposed to be good for reading comprehension. | talk |
| Minori | Yes, I see...!<br>Then, Shizuku, how about you? | talk |
| Shizuku | Whenever I'm reading my textbook, I try to keep an eye out for conjunctions. | talk |
| Minori | Conjunctions? Like “but” and “so” and stuff? | talk |
| Shizuku | Yes. While I like reading stories, I can't say that I'm very good with critical texts... | talk |
| Shizuku | But I feel that I understand them better when I actively keep an eye out for conjunctions. | talk |
| Airi | Wait, Shizuku, that sounds like a pretty high-level technique. | talk |
| Shizuku | Hehe. It's not that hard actually. | talk |
| Shizuku | For instance, when someone says, “I think this or that,” and follows it up with a “because”, that tells me they're about to explain their reasoning. | talk |
| Shizuku | However, if they were to use a “but”, that likely means they're about to say something to the contrary. | talk |
| Minori & Airi | Whoa! | talk |
| Minori | That's amazing, Shizuku!<br>I'm gonna try doing that the next time one of those essays shows up in my textbook! | talk |
| Airi | I have to agree.<br>The speed at which someone can digest text would change a ton depending on whether or not they did what you just said. | talk |
| Haruka | Did you come up with this technique by yourself, Shizuku? | talk |
| Shizuku | No, a teacher explained it to me. | talk |
| Shizuku | As a result, I'm having much less trouble reading and analyzing such texts. | talk |
| Haruka | Coming up with your own ways to make studying easier is really important, but so is asking the teachers for help. | talk |

| Minori | I really can't take them for granted. | talk |
| Minori | And I always feel inspired to do my best for them. | talk |
| Minori | Our fans have become a huge source of hope for me. | talk |
| KAITO | *Nod* | talk |
| Minori | So, because they've given me so much encouragement, I want to spread lots of hope. | talk |
| Minori | It'd be so great if we could keep giving each other hope as I continue to work as an idol. | talk |
| KAITO | I see...<br>That dream made you feel all these things? | talk |
| KAITO | I'm absolutely certain that you'll achieve your goal, Minori. You're going to become an idol who receives hope from her fans and gives hope back to them. | talk |
| Minori | KAITO... | talk |
| KAITO | The others and I will also give you all the support we can moving forward. | talk |
| KAITO | So, if you ever need our assistance, please feel free to ask! | talk |
| Minori | Thank you, I will! | talk |
| Minori | Alright, in order to spread hope to lots of people, I'm gonna keep doing more and more! | talk |
| Minori | So, KAITO, please watch me perform that dance one more time! | talk |
| Minori | I feel like I can put on an even better performance now! | talk |
| KAITO | Of course! I'll keep helping until you feel completely satisfied, Minori! | talk |
| KAITO | Let's work together to produce the best performance we can! | talk |
| Minori | Yes, thank you very much! | talk |
| Minori | Okay, let me get to my position... | talk |
| Minori | I'm ready, KAITO! Start the music! | talk |
|  | bgm00000 | Sound |
